Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in homological theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 152 and the invariant I(A,B) = 249 mod 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the homological space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 152 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 249. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The homological variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 303 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 420 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 303 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the homological structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 420. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 216.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the homological space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 340 and the convergence rate is O(355/n^1).

Proof: The optimality condition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 340 + O(1/n^355). Summing over all modes and applying the homological optimality condition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
